HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000452/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_02f88dcebc2a9e05080f","text":"3. **Choosing the better option.** Lexical evidence: خ ي ر B003 (choosing/seeking better), ء ث ر B005 in 87:16 (preferring), ي س ر B007 in 87:8 (apportioned lots). Structural cue: bad preference is followed by the better object. Assigned role: خير corrects إيثار by identifying the truly preferable share. Abductive move: ethical life is a selection problem under temporal pressure. Reading change: the ayah is an invitation to reorder choice, not only information about afterlife.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1846fa90e5970905f086","text":"2. **Remainder versus refuse.** Lexical evidence: ب ق ي B002 (remaining portion/residue), غ ث و B001/B002/B004 in 87:5, ح و ي B001/B006. Structural cue: chaff/refuse appears before the enduring afterlife. Assigned role: بقاء names valuable remainder; غثاء names worthless remainder. Abductive move: the surah distinguishes two kinds of residue after change: enduring good and discarded waste. Reading change: \"more enduring\" gains material contrast.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1a935ca4b5a77af134a6","text":"87:18–19 gave `ب ق ي/B002` a concrete retrospective image: earlier written sheets are a surviving remainder that still carries usable content. That documentary persistence also corrected duration alone as the point of `أَبْقَىٰ`; what remains is communicative and beneficial.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_21ab19bf42dde0816591","text":"3. **What endures is preserved good, not mere duration.** `ب ق ي/B003` gives sparing and keeping alive through mercy, while `خ ي ر/B005` gives beneficence and gift. This pairing is activated by the non-beneficial suspension of 87:13: endless continuation there is not called good, whereas here benefit and preservation are grammatically joined. Sparing supplies the preserving action and gift supplies its quality. I infer that `أَبْقَىٰ` is duration under beneficent preservation. The ayah distinguishes valuable continuance from mere inability to end.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2aceb3a25a4c74ac3496","text":"4. **Choice language corrected by خير.** Lexical evidence: خير B003 choosing/seeking better option; أثر B005 preferring in 87:16. Structural cue: one ayah says \"you prefer\"; the next says \"better.\" Assigned role: خير is the objective better option against subjective preference. Abductive move: the packet supplies choice and preference; I infer a correction of agency. Reading change: the ayah confronts the audience's choosing with the truly choice-worthy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B002","root_000452/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4ad618657c1a71fa49a0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:17:2\",\"root_000452\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:17:3\",\"root_000142\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kalıcı olanın bıraktığı pay, kurumuş otun değersiz artığından farklı olarak kullanılabilir ve iyi bir kalıntıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001","root_000019/B002","root_000142/B001","root_000452/B001","root_000452/B002","root_000452/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5fb5f30cfb317d5bbddf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:17:1\",\"root_000019\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"87:17:2\",\"root_000452\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]],[\"87:17:3\",\"root_000142\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ayet, önceki tercihi zaman, nitelik ve süre bakımından düzelten bir seçim ölçüsü kurar: Sonraki olan hem daha değerlidir hem daha uzun sürer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_70e757778c46de4366bb","text":"87:18-19 gave this claim documentary weight: \"better and more enduring\" is not only this surah's assertion but belongs to former sheets. 87:5 made أبقى surprisingly concrete by contrast with waste-residue.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B001","root_000142/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_92b291bbda49040a5276","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:17:3\",\"root_000142\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hemen sonraki eski sayfalar, kalıcılığın geçmişten bugüne ulaşan öğretici ve aktarılabilir bir kalıntı da ürettiğini gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9dd45e763113ed23861a","text":"1. **The later is better and more remaining.** Lexical evidence: أخر B001 later/other than first and B002 deferring to later time; خير B001 desired good and B002 chosen excellence; بقي B001 persistence/remaining. Structural cue: direct contrast with الدنيا of 87:16. Assigned role: الآخرة reorders time; خير evaluates quality; أبقى evaluates duration. Abductive move: the packet supplies laterness, goodness, and persistence; I infer a two-axis correction: better in kind and longer in duration. Reading change: the ayah is a value calculus, not a vague consolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a7bd6ac59b72b1a15032","text":"2. **Remaining portion versus consumed pasture.** Lexical evidence: بقي B002 remaining portion/residue; غثو B001/B002 refuse/residue in 87:5; رعي B001 pasture. Structural cue: the surah's natural cycle produced perishable residue; now \"remaining\" is predicated of the afterlife. Assigned role: أبقى gives a positive remainder unlike waste. Abductive move: the packet supplies both residue and durable remainder; I infer contrast between leftover chaff and preserved good. Reading change: what remains is not all equal: afterlife remains as good, not refuse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b4254a3d4d405c6b166f","text":"87:18-19 made \"الآخرة خير وأبقى\" part of an older archive, strengthening it as inherited wisdom. It also made بقي B001 echo textual persistence: the teaching remains in sheets just as the afterlife remains beyond الدنيا.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b64c5fa08aa19033a38e","text":"1. **The comparison repairs the preceding choice rule.** `ء خ ر/B001` gives what is later or other than the first, `خ ي ر/B003` gives choosing or seeking the better option, and `ب ق ي/B001` gives persistence rather than perishing. Juxtaposition with `تُؤْثِرُونَ ... ٱلدُّنْيَا` in 87:16 turns the three predicates into selection criteria. Laterness identifies the alternative; goodness ranks it; persistence tests the ranking through time. I infer that 87:17 is corrective evaluation of the earlier choice. The ayah reads as a decision rule, not only a description of two realms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001","root_000452/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c349e4dea9bc86a8b57f","text":"1. **Later is better because it remains.** Lexical evidence: ء خ ر B001/B002 (later/other; deferred), خ ي ر B001/B002 (good and chosen excellence), ب ق ي B001 (persistence). Structural cue: direct contrast with الدنيا in 87:16. Assigned role: آخِرة supplies temporal distance; خير evaluates it; أبقى gives ontological durability. Abductive move: what is deferred can outrank what is immediate when it remains. Reading change: the afterlife is not merely later; its laterness is part of its superiority.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000142/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c9dd4950c008ebbccd9b","text":"2. **Endurance leaves a transmissible remainder.** `ب ق ي/B002` is the remaining portion or residue. The written sheets in 87:18–19 immediately provide a material example of something surviving from an earlier time. The residue branch gives `أَبْقَىٰ` the role of leaving evidence and usable content after an originating moment has passed. I infer that the sheets exemplify, rather than exhaust, that endurance. Structural cue: opening mercy and life-preservation branches return after the warning. Assigned role: أبقى can imply merciful preservation from destruction. Abductive move: the packet supplies sparing and mercy; I infer that the afterlife's remaining quality includes being kept by mercy.
